import time
import numpy as np

def _as_valid(arr, max_len=None):
   try: a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   except: return None
   if a.size ==0: return None
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   if a.size>0 and a.sum()>0.01: return a
   return None

def _uniform_from_parent(parent=None, length=None):
   if parent is not None:
      l=int(parent.size)
      if 1 <= l <= 1_000_000: return np.ones(l)
   if length is not None and length>0: return np.ones(int(length))
   return np.ones(1)

```python
import time
import numpy as np

def _finite_nonnegative_array(value):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(value, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not a.size:
        return None
    return a

def _safe_uniform_length():
    parent = None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
       parent = _finite_nonnegative_array(globals()['height_sequence_1'])
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
       length = int(parent.size)
    else:
       length = 1
    # keep length manageable for dense convexolver and practical harness evaluation
    if length > 10000:
        # 6962 known fit; if parent larger choose moderate.
        length = 6962 if length >= 6962 else 1000
    if length < 1: length=1
    return length

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        parent = None
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            parent = _finite_nonnegative_array(globals()['height_sequence_1'])
        if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
            out = [1.0]
        else:
            # The exact objective has lower bound 2 for nonnegative finite sequences:
            # 2n max(f*f)/(sum f)^2 >= 2n sum f^2/(sum f)^2 >= 2.
            # A constant sequence attains 2. Use parent's length when safely available.
            n = int(parent.size)
            if n > 20000: # safe
                n = 6962 if n > 6962 else 20000
            # If budget allows, validate. But uniform no need. Maybe evaluate parent? Not needed.
            # choose 1.0; scale arbitrary.
            out = [1.0] * n
            # Optional: if parent is already valid and maybe exact score <2 due roundoff? We can evaluate parent and candidate with time guard.
            # But parent convolve O(n^2) maybe okay for 6962. Do it to preserve possible sub-2 numeric?
            remaining = deadline? Need compute.
    except:
        return [1.0]
```

```
    if not seq: return inf
    try:
       s=evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
       return inf
    return float(s) if np.isfinite(s) else inf

def propose_candidate(...):
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np

_MAX_RETURN_LEN = 6962
_EVAL_LEN_LIMIT = 10000

def _clean_array(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    return a

def _safe_score(seq):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        s = float(s)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return float('inf')
    return s

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    if deadline < start:
        deadline = start
    parent = None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
        parent = _clean_array(globals()['height_sequence_1'])
    best_seq = None
    best_score = float('inf')

    # Candidate 1: parent if finite, nonempty, safe length, and time remains.
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 1 and parent.size <= _EVAL_LEN_LIMIT and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            pseq = [float(x) for x in parent.tolist()]
        except Exception:
            pseq = None
        if pseq:
            s = _safe_score(pseq)
            if s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_seq = pseq

    # Candidate 2: constant sequence; provably optimal for the evaluator.
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 1:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > _MAX_RETURN_LEN:
            n = _MAX_RETURN_LEN
    else:
        n = 1
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
    cseq = [1.0] * n
    if cseq:
       s=_safe_score(cseq)
       if s < best_score:
          best_score=s; best_seq=cseq
    # If no finite score (e.g., evaluate_sequence unavailable? but it is), fallback constant.
    if best_seq is None or not best_seq or not all(np.isfinite(x) for x in best_seq[:1000])? Use np? best_seq list maybe large. Just return cseq if invalid.
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq = cseq if cseq else [1.0]
    # Optional final validate if time.
    if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= _EVAL_LEN_LIMIT and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
        fs = _safe_score(best_seq)
        if fs < best_score:
            best_score = fs
    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = [1.0]
    # Ensure coefficients finite and within cap? The evaluator clips but return finite.
    # For list length 6962, converting to np maybe.
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            best_seq = cseq if cseq else [1.0]
        else:
            arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
            best_seq = [float(v) for v in arr.tolist()]
    except Exception:
        best_seq = cseq if cseq else [1.0]
    if not best_seq:
        best_seq=[1.0]
    return best_seq
```

Let's draft final code:
```python
import time
import numpy as np

_MAX_LEN = 6962
_EVAL_LIMIT = 10000

def _clean(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def _score(seq):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        s = float(s)
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    return s if np.isfinite(s) else float('inf')

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        remaining = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 3.0)
    except Exception:
        remaining = 0.0
    deadline = start + remaining

    parent = None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
        parent = _clean(globals()['height_sequence_1'])

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 1:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > _MAX_LEN:
            n = _MAX_LEN
    else:
        n = 1
    if n < 1:
        n = 1

    # Constant sequence attains the lower bound 2 for the given evaluator.
    best_seq = [1.0] * n
    best_score = float('inf')
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best_score = _score(best_seq)

    if (parent is not None and 1 <= parent.size <= _EVAL_LIMIT and
            time.monotonic() < deadline):
        try:
            pseq = [float(v) for v in parent.tolist()]
        except Exception:
            pseq = None
        if pseq:
            pscore = _score(pseq)
            if pscore < best_score:
                best_seq = pseq
                best_score = pscore

    # If best sequence is not usable, fall back to short constant.
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            raise ValueError
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        best_seq = [float(v) for v in arr.tolist()]
    except Exception:
        best_seq = [1.0] * min(n, 1)

    if not best_seq:
        best_seq = [1.0]
    return best_seq
```

Let's think about exact proof again: Is max_b necessarily at least sumsq? In full convolution output length 2n-1. The zero-lag autocorrelation is at index n-1. `np.convolve(a,a)` index n-1 = sum_{i=0}^{n-1} a[i]*a[n-1 - (n-1-i)]? Let's derive: For 1D convolution c[k]=sum_m a[m] b[k-m]. With a=b. For k=n-1, m from max(0,k-n+1)=0 to min(n-1,k)=n-1. b[n-1-m] valid. c[n-1]=sum_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m]a[n-1-m]? That's reversed, not sum squares necessarily! Wait big error! Let's design an adaptive mirror descent / active set algorithm that can run for n=2048/6962. We need ensure it improves.

Detailed algorithm (continuous discrete p):
- Input initial p (sum 1, positive).
- Maintain z = log p.
- At each outer iteration:
  - c = convolve(p,p); J=max(c); score=2nJ.
  - Choose active lags: top K where c >= J - delta*J (e.g., delta=1e-3) or top K= min(10, #{c>0.9J}). To ensure enough, include top at least 3 and up to maybe 20. Could include all with c > 0.95J.
  - Compute h = average gradient over active: h = sum shifts / K.
  - Normalize h: h_scale = max(h) maybe (if all zeros, stop). Direction d = h / h_scale (nonnegative O(1)).
  - We want move p in direction reducing h. Mirror update: z_new = z - η d - (max of? recenter). Actually d may have large at some indices; we can subtract mean to reduce effect? Since sum p fixed, only differences matter. Subgradient of constraint ∑? If we update z with -η d, the log-sum ensures sum fixed; adding constant to d doesn't matter. To center, use d0 = d - mean(d). But mirror gradient descent on simplex: update p_i ∝ p_i exp(-η d_i). If d has large mean, it uniformly shrinks then renormalizes; adding constant doesn't change p. So use centered d to reduce step magnitude: d = h - mean(h) (or h - median). Then z -= η d. η can be larger. But if h not centered? The gradient of J under sum constraint should be constrained to tangent space; subtract mean. Yes.
  - Adaptive η: Try a set of step sizes, e.g., baseline η0 scaled by? Since p changes; maybe use `eta = alpha / (np.max(d_abs)+eps)`? If d centered O(1), eta = 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0,... Try increasing until score decreases? Since nonconvex, can allow increase if new score < best_score? We need progress. Use line search: for eta in [1e-4,3e-4,... up to 10], compute new p, score. Choose first with score < current score - eps or best? If none, reduce global scale and maybe use a random perturbation.
  - But computing score for many eta each iter costly; convolve O(n^2). For n=2048, maybe okay few. For 6962, maybe expensive. Could use surrogate: because h is subgradient of active, approximate new J? Or limit tries to 3.
- To escape local minima, use random restarts / perturbations: add noise to z, rescale, perhaps reverse, random smooth basis. But time.

Implement objective for L-BFGS:
```
def make_fun(y, n, tau_base, interp):
  def fun(v):
     f = positive interpolation from exp(v)
     p = f / f.sum()
     c = np.convolve(p,p)
     J = c.max()
     if J <=0: return 1e-300, zeros
     yval = (c/J) - 1  # <=0, max 0
     # logsumexp(yval) stable: max=0
     s = np.sum(np.exp(yval, where=yval > -50))  (many zeros)
     val = J + tau * np.log(s)
     # gradient wrt v:
     alpha = exp(yval)/s
     h = 0
     loop over active where alpha>eps: h += alpha[k]*shift(p,k) / K? Actually gradient of F = sum alpha_k * grad(c_k) (no factor? c_k gradient is 2 shift as above). Our shift function should return 2*avg? We'll include 2.
     Then chain to p: dp_idx = h_i
     dp/dv_j = derivative of p wrt v.
```

Building sparse A for primal:

```
data=[]; indices=[]; indptr=[0]
for r in range(2*n-1):
   c_lo=max(0,r-n+1); c_hi=min(n-1,r)+1
   cols=np.arange(c_lo,c_hi)
   vals=f[r-cols]
   data.append(vals); indices.append(cols)
   indptr.append(indptr[-1]+len(cols))
A=scipy.sparse.csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data), np.concatenate(indices), indptr), shape=(2n-1,n))
```

Algorithm active_LP_update(f, n, M, active_lags):
- Build A (len(A) x n) float64 dense or sparse. For each k: row = zeros(n); lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(n,k+1); src indices k-lo.. for row[lo:hi] 2? Wait constraint (f*g)_k = ∑_i f_{k-i} g_i. Row coefficient for variable i is f_{k-i}. This is similar to grad but without factor 2. Use slice assignment:
```
row = np.zeros(n)
lo = max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n, k+1)
end = k-(hi-1); start=k-lo
if end==0: row[lo:hi]=f[start::-1]
else: row[lo:hi]=f[start:end-1:-1]
```

Implement helper:
```
_fft_len = None
def conv_fft(p):
    L = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    # Due FFT small negative, clip 0
    return c
```
Memory: F length L/2+1 complex ~16385*16=262KB; fine. For each candidate, one rfft + inverse ~ fast (<1ms?). This is much faster than direct. We can use this for internal objective. Detailed `refine(f, n, max_iters, deadline, use_lp=True)`:
Inputs f sum1 nonnegative. Returns best f, best_J (surrogate exact? track via FFT). It will use exact occasionally? We can track surrogate, final exact.
Pseudo:
```
def refine(f, n, max_iters, deadline, rng, K=100, use_lp=True):
    f = f.copy(); f += eps; f/=f.sum()
    c = conv_fft(f); J=c.max(); best=f.copy(); bestJ=J
    it=0
    lp_fail_streak=0
    while it<max_iters and time<deadline:
        it+=1
        c = conv_fft(f); if c.max() < J - 1e-15: ... update
        J=c.max()
        # active set
        active = choose_active(c, n, rng, K)
        g = None
        if use_lp:
            A=M=b=...
            try:
               res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=..., method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':?})
            except: res=None
            if res.success and res.x is not None and res.x.sum() > f.sum()*(1+1e-8):
                g=clip(res.x,...); g/=g.sum()
                lp_fail_streak=0
            else: lp_fail_streak++
        if g is None:
            # mirror gradient using active
            h=compute_gradient_average(f, active)
            d=h - h.mean()
            if norm d small: # random perturb
                f = perturb(f, rng); continue
            # try steps
            improved=False
            for eta in [2.0,1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
                p = f * np.exp(-eta*d / max(1e-300, np.abs(d).max()))
                p = np.abs(p)+eps; p/=p.sum()
                Jp = conv_fft(p).max()
                if Jp < J - 1e-15:
                    f=p; J=Jp; improved=True; break
            if not improved:
                # random block perturb
                f = block_perturb(f, rng)
                J=conv_fft(f).max()
        else:
            improved=False
            for t in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
                p = (1-t)*f + t*g
                p = np.abs(p)+eps; p/=p.sum()
                Jp=conv_fft(p).max()
                if Jp < J - 1e-15:
                   f=p; J=Jp; improved=True; break
            if not improved:
                # maybe accept small random perturb to escape; keep best
                f = block_perturb(f, rng, small)
                J=conv_fft(f).max()
        if J < bestJ:
            bestJ=J; best=f.copy()
            # periodically exact validate if time
            if it % 50 ==0 or J < current_exact? : evaluate best list maybe.
    return best,bestJ
```

Let's write building shapes:
```
def smooth_density(n, rng, m=32, smooth=0.5):
    x = rng.normal(0,1,m)
    # maybe trend
    ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    trend = np.linspace(0,1,m)
    y = 1 + 0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*trend/rng.uniform(0.5,2)+ph) + rng.normal(0,0.3,m)
    y = np.abs(y)+0.1
    # interpolate to n
    xi = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    xg = (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    f = np.interp(xi, xg, y)
    f = f**2  # emphasize smooth? no
    return f/f.sum()
```
Deterministic shapes:
```
def base_shapes(n):
  t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  shapes=[]
  for a in [-0.9,-0.5,0.5,0.9]: shapes.append(1+a*t)
  for lam in [-3,-1,1,3]: shapes.append(np.exp(lam*t))
  shapes.append(1+0.7*np.cos(4*np.pi*t)); etc.
  # normalize clip
```

Let's refine active LP details with dense matrix:
```
def solve_active_lp(f, active, M, high=100.0, bound_obj=None):
    K=len(active); n=len(f)
    A=np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
        k=int(k)
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
        if lo>=hi: continue
        start=k-lo; end=k-(hi-1)
        if end==0:
            A[idx, lo:hi] = f[start::-1]
        else:
            A[idx, lo:hi] = f[start:end-1:-1]
    cvec=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(K,M), bounds=Bounds(0,high), method='highs')
    if res.success and res.x is not None: return res.x
    return None
```
Check row assignment for k>=n: e.g. n=3,k=2: lo=0? max(0,2-3+1=0), hi=min(3,3)=3, start=2, end=k-(hi-1)=2-2=0 -> row=f[2::-1]=[f2,f1,f0]. (f*g)_2 = f0*g2 + f1*g1 + f2*g0? Wait variable g_i coefficient f_{k-i}: for i=0 coefficient f2, i=1 f1, i=2 f0 -> [f2,f1,f0]. Good.
For k=4: lo=max(0,2), hi=3, start=4-2=2, end=4-(2)=2? hi-1=2 => end=2, slice f[2:1:-1] -> [f2]; row[2]=f2. Variable g2 coefficient f_{4-2=2}. Good.

Active LP uses `M` maybe max conv of f. Since f sum1. b=M. If f has zeros, rows all zero? Not.

Actually g_norm sum1. We can also candidate g alone; if no improve, random.

```
def random_perturb(f, rng, sigma=0.1):
   m=32
   noise=np.interp(np.linspace(0.5/n,1-0.5/n,n), (np.arange(m)+0.5)/m, rng.normal(0,1,m))
   p=f*np.exp(sigma*noise)
```

Upsampling density:
```
def upsample_density(p, n_new, method='linear'):
   if p.size==n_new: return normalize(p)
   # density h = p.size * p (since sum p=1)
   h = p * p.size
   # cell centers
   x_old=(np.arange(p.size)+0.5)/p.size
   x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
   h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
   h_new=np.clip(h_new,0,None)+1e-12
   return h_new/h_new.sum()
```
If p has zeros, interp okay.

Parent downsampling if parent length different: use same with h=p.size*p. If parent length 6962 and n_f=6962, direct normalize.

```
import time, numpy as np, scipy.optimize as opt, scipy.fft as fft

def _normalize(p): p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.abs(p); p[p<0]=0; s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(len)/len?; return p/s

def _valid_density(logger): return array sum1 finite.

_FFT_LEN={}
def _conv(p):
   n=len(p); L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length(); F=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]; np.maximum(c,0,out=c); return c
def _score_ft(p): return _conv(p).max()
```
For memory, np.fft overwrites. Use `np.maximum`.

`_build_rows(f, active, n)`:
Need ensure active list unique ints.
```
def _build_lp_matrix(f, active, n):
    A=np.zeros((len(active), n), float)
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
       k=int(k)
       if k<0 or k>=2*n-1: continue
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
       if lo>=hi: continue
       start=k-lo; end=k-(hi-1)
       if end==0:
          A[r, lo:hi] = f[start::-1]
       else:
          A[r, lo:hi] = f[start:end-1:-1]
    return A
```
Check if end-1 negative when end=0 handled. For k=? If end=0, slice f[start::-1] includes to 0. If end>0, slice f[start:end-1:-1] includes down to end. Good.

`_lp_update(f, active, M, bounds, max_full=False)`:

```
# If some row all zero? remove? Not necessary but linprog may warn. We can filter rows if row.sum()<=0.
row_has = A.sum(axis=1)>0
A=A[row_has]; b=b[row_has]
if A.size==0: return None
try:
  res=opt.linprog(-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*np.ones(A.shape[0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
except Exception: return None
if res.success and res.x is not None:
   g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s
return None
```
Note b is M (same). If M=0, return None.

`choose_active`:
```
def _choose_active(c, n, rng, K, use_full=False, random_frac=0.2):
   m=2*n-1
   if use_full: return np.arange(m)
   top = np.argsort(c)[-K]
   spread_count = max(8, K//4)
   spread = np.linspace(0, m-1, spread_count).astype(int)
   low = np.arange(0, min(m, K//4))
   # maybe central constraints near n-1 (important)
   center = n-1
   center_idx = np.arange(max(0,center-5), min(m,center+6))
   allv = unique(top, spread, low, center_idx)
   # add random if time
   if allv.size < K:
      rand = rng.integers(0,m,K)
      allv=unique(allv,rand)
   return allv[:K*2?]
```
Need max total. K_total maybe K. If we add too many, LP bigger. Let target total T = K (e.g., 100). We can select top half, spread half. Simpler:
```

spread = np.linspace(0,m-1, T-top_K).astype(int)
act = unique(top, spread)
```

`refine` details:

```
def _refine(p, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=1000, K=100, use_full=False, verbose=False):
    p=_normalize(p)
    p += 1e-12; p/=p.sum()
    J=_score_ft(p)
    best=p.copy(); bestJ=J
    high=2.0
    no_imp=0
    lp_streak=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       c=_conv(p)
       J=c.max()
       if J < bestJ - 1e-15:
           bestJ=J; best=p.copy(); no_imp=0
       active = full if use_full else choose
       g=None
       if not use_full and J>0:
           M=J
           try LP using active, M
       if g is None:
           # gradient fallback
       else: line search
       # if p J not improved frequently, reset/perturb
```

Gradient fallback implementation:
```
def _gradient_dir(p, active):
   n=len(p); h=np.zeros(n)
   cnt=0
   for k in active:
      k=int(k); lo...; add 2*shift? factor irrelevant.
      if end==0: h[lo:hi] += p[start::-1]
      else: h[lo:hi] += p[start:end-1:-1]
      cnt+=1
   if cnt: h/=cnt
   d=h - h.mean()
   return d
```
This is gradient of active coefficients without factor 2. For direction negative.

Line search for gradient:
```
d = _gradient_dir(p, active)
s = np.max(np.abs(d))
if s==0: perturb
for eta in [1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
   pnew=p*np.exp(-eta*d/s)
   pnew=np.abs(pnew); pnew += eps; pnew/=pnew.sum()
   Jp=_score_ft(pnew)
   if Jp < J - threshold: accept; break
```
Should we use `d` or `-d`? We want reduce h, so p ∝ exp(-η d). Since h large where coefficient uses p_i; reducing p_i reduces coefficient. Good.

Random perturb:

```
def _perturb(p,rng,sigma=0.1):
   m=32
   x_old=(arange(m)+0.5)/m; x_new=(arange(n)+0.5)/n
   noise=np.interp(x_new,x_old,rng.normal(0,1,m))
   pnew=p*np.exp(sigma*noise)
   pnew+=eps; pnew/=sum
```
Use sigma decays? 0.05-0.2.

```
def _make_starts(n, parent, rng, count=6):

   t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   for a in [-0.7,0.7,-0.3,0.3]: starts.append((1+a*t))
   for lam in [-4,4,-2,2]: starts.append(np.exp(lam*t))
   shapes add cos 2π,4π
   # parent if available: if parent.size==n, normalize; else upsample
```

Let's design `refine` loop more concretely:
```
def _refine(p, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=200, K=96, use_full=False):
    p = _normalize(p); p = p + 1e-12; p/=p.sum()
    c = _conv(p); J = float(c.max())
    best = p.copy(); bestJ=J
    no_imp=0
    reset_count=0
    lp_fail=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
        # c already corresponds to p. At top after previous update, recompute if not.
        active = _all_lags if use_full else _choose_active(c,len(p),rng,K)
        g = None
        if J > 1e-300:
            A = _build_lp_matrix(p, active, n)
            row_mask = A.sum(axis=1) > 1e-300
            if row_mask.any():
                M_lp = J*(1+1e-8)+1e-15
                try:
                   res = opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A[row_mask], b_ub=np.full(int(row_mask.sum()), M_lp), bounds=(0.0,1.0), method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
                except Exception:
                   res = None
                if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
                    g = np.abs(res.x); s = g.sum()
                    if s > 1e-12:
                       g /= s
                       if np.sum(g) > 0:
                           lp_fail=0
                    else: g=None
        if g is None:
            h = _gradient_average(p, active, n)
            d = h - np.mean(h)
            dscale = np.max(np.abs(d))
            improved=False
            if dscale > 1e-15:
                for eta in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                    pnew = p*np.exp(-eta*d/dscale)
                    pnew = np.abs(pnew)+1e-12; pnew/=pnew.sum()

                    if Jnew < J - 1e-15:
                        p,pnew? p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; improved=True; break
            if not improved:
                # small random perturb

                cnew=_conv(pnew); Jnew=...

                    p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
                    if no_imp > 20:
                        # reset to best with perturb
                        p = _perturb(best, rng, 0.15)
                        c = _conv(p); J=c.max(); no_imp=0
                        reset_count +=1

                    continue
        else:

            for t in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):

                pnew = np.abs(pnew)+1e-12; pnew/=pnew.sum()

                lp_fail=0
            else:

                if lp_fail > 5:
                   # try gradient from active
                   h=...; try once maybe
                no_imp += 1
                if no_imp > 25: reset...

            best=p.copy(); bestJ=J; no_imp=0
        elif no_imp > 0: no_imp +=1
    return best,bestJ
```

```
def _slice_info(n):
  info=[]
  for k in range(2*n-1):
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
    if lo>=hi: info.append(None); continue
    start=k-lo; end=k-(hi-1)
    info.append((lo,hi,start,end, end==0))
  return info
```

```
def _random_smooth(n, rng, m=48):

     amp=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)
     freq=rng.integers(1,6)
     ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
     y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
   y=np.abs(y)+0.05
   xi=cell centers; yi=interp
```

Let's write code skeleton now mentally:

```
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as opt

_EPS = 1e-12
_INFO_CACHE = {}
_FFT_CACHE = {}

def _slice_info(n):
   if n not in _INFO_CACHE:
      info=[]
      for k in range(2*n-1):
         lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
         if lo>=hi:
            info.append(None)
         else:
            start=k-lo; end=k-(hi-1)
            info.append((lo,hi,start,end,end==0))
      _INFO_CACHE[n]=info
   return _INFO_CACHE[n]

def _fft_len(n):
   key=n
   if key not in _FFT_CACHE:
      L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
      _FFT_CACHE[key]=L
   return _FFT_CACHE[key]

def _conv_fft(p):
   n=p.size
   L=_fft_len(n)
   F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
   # clean tiny negatives
   np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
   return c

def _score_ft(p):
   return float(_conv_fft(p).max())

def _normalize(p):
   arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
   arr=np.abs(arr)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)/max(1,arr.size)
   return arr/s
```

`_fill_row(A_row, f, info)`? Use in build matrix and gradient:
```
def _apply_row(f, info, out, scale=1.0):
   lo,hi,start,end,zeroend = info
   if zeroend: out[lo:hi] = f[start::-1]
   else: out[lo:hi] = f[start:end-1:-1]
```
Need scale for gradient? no.

```
return A
```

```
  return None

```

Let's write refine cleanly:
```
def _refine(p, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=200, K=80, use_full=False):
    p=_normalize(p); p += _EPS; p/=p.sum()
    c=_conv_fft(p); J=float(c.max())
    best=p.copy(); bestJ=J
    no_imp=0; lp_stale=0
    info_cache? not needed
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
        active = np.arange(2*n-1) if use_full else _choose_active(c,n,rng,K,use_full)
        improved=False
        # LP direction
        oldJ=J
        if J > 1e-300 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
            g=_lp_solution(p, active, J*(1+1e-8)+1e-15, p.size)
            if g is not None:
                best_t=None; best_J=oldJ; best_p=None
                for t in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8: break
                    pnew=(1-t)*p + t*g
                    pnew=np.abs(pnew)+_EPS; pnew/=pnew.sum()
                    cnew=_conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < oldJ - 1e-15:
                        improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
                lp_stale=0 if improved else lp_stale+1
        # gradient direction if still no improvement and LP stale or no LP
        if not improved and (lp_stale >= 2 or time.monotonic() < deadline - 8):
            h=_gradient_average(p, active, n)
            d=h - h.mean()
            ds=float(d.max())+float((-d).max())? use absmax
            if ds > 1e-15:
                for eta in (2.0,1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8: break
                    pnew=p*np.exp(-eta*d/ds)
                    pnew=np.abs(pnew)+_EPS; pnew/=pnew.sum()
                    cnew=_conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < J - 1e-15:
                        improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
        if not improved:
            # stochastic small move, but try to improve current, not just random
            for sigma in (0.08,0.15,0.05):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8: break
                pnew=_perturb(p, rng, sigma)
                cnew=_conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                if Jnew < J - 1e-15:
                    improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
        if improved:
            no_imp=0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > 25:
                # reset to best with perturbation
                p=_perturb(best, rng, 0.2)
                c=_conv_fft(p); J=float(c.max())
                no_imp=0
                if J > bestJ * (1.01) and no_imp reset count? need count
```

```
   p=_perturb(best,rng,0.2); ...; no_imp=0; reset_count+=1
   if reset_count>10: break
```

Now, `_initial_starts`:
```
def _initial_starts(n, parent, rng, count=6):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    starts=[]
    # parent
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       h=parent*parent.size
       if parent.size != n:
          x_old=(arange(parent.size)+0.5)/parent.size; x_new=(arange(n)+0.5)/n; h=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
       h=np.abs(h)+1e-6; s=h.sum(); starts.append(h/s)
    # deterministic
    for a in (-0.8,0.8,-0.35,0.35):
       f=1+a*t
       if f.min()>0: starts.append(f/f.sum())
    for lam in (-4.0,4.0,-2.0,2.0):
       f=np.exp(lam*t); starts.append(f/f.sum())
    f=1+0.7*np.cos(4*np.pi*t); if min>0 starts
    f=1+0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*t); min? sin can -0.7, 0.3 positive.
    # random smooth
    for i in range(count):
       starts.append(_random_smooth(n,rng))
    # trim to unique? Normalize
    out=[]; seen=0
    for f in starts:
       f=_normalize(f)
       if f.size!=n: continue
       if np.std(f) < 1e-6/n? uniform; add small trend? maybe keep but not useful.
       out.append(f)
       if len(out) >= count+4: break
    return out
```

`_random_smooth`:
```
def _random_smooth(n, rng, m=48):
    m=min(max(8,m), n)
    x=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    y=np.ones(m)
    for _ in range(3):
       amp=rng.uniform(0.15,0.75)
       freq=int(rng.integers(1,7))
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
    y=np.abs(y)+0.05
    x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    f=np.interp(x_new, x, y)
    f=f**2 # maybe positive
    return _normalize(f)
```
If n small, m=n.

Now, upsample:
```
def _upsample(p, n_new):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size == n_new: return _normalize(p)
    h=p*p.size
    if h.size == 1:
       return np.full(n_new, 1.0/n_new)
    x_old=(np.arange(h.size)+0.5)/h.size
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h)
    h_new=np.clip(h_new,0.0,None)+1e-12
    return h_new/h_new.sum()
```

```
def _clean_parent():
  try:
    arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float).ravel()
  except: return None
  arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
  arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
  if arr.size==0: return None
  return arr
```

```
def _apply_row(f, info, out):
    lo, hi, start, end, zero_end = info
    if zero_end:
        out[lo:hi] = f[start::-1]
    else:
        out[lo:hi] = f[start:end-1:-1]
```
Test k=1,n=3: lo=0,hi=2,start=1,end=0 zero -> out[0:2]=f[1::-1]=[f1,f0]. row coefficients for (f*g)_1: i=0 f1, i=1 f0 -> good.
k=2,n=3: lo=0,hi=3,start=2,end=0 -> [f2,f1,f0], good.
k=3,n=3: lo=1,hi=3,start=3-1=2,end=3-(2)=1 zero? end=1 not zero -> out[1:3]=f[2:0:-1] -> indices 2,1 => [f2,f1]. Full row variables i=1 coeff f2? k=3,i=1 => f2; i=2=>f1. out[1]=f2,out[2]=f1; out[0]=0. Good.

`_build_a`:
```
def _build_a(f, active, n):
    info = _slice_info(n)
    A = np.zeros((len(active), n), dtype=float)
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
       inf = info[int(k)]
       if inf is None: continue
       _apply_row(f, inf, A[r])
    return A
```
If active is list of numpy ints, fine.

`_choose_active` if K==0 returns all? In refine, use_full flag K==0. In `_choose_active`, can handle `if K is None or K <=0: return np.arange(m)`. Good.
```
def _choose_active(c,n,rng,K=0):
   m=2*n-1
   if K<=0 or K>=m: return np.arange(m)
   top = int(max(24, min(m//2, K//2)))
   top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-top:]
   rest=K-top
   sp=max(8, rest)
   sp_idx=np.linspace(0,m-1,sp).astype(int)
   act=np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, sp_idx)))
   if act.size < K:
      rand=rng.integers(0,m,K)
      act=np.unique(np.concatenate((act, rand)))
   return act[:K]
```
If top+sp >K, final unique maybe >K? sp=K-top so sum ~K, unique may less or maybe top/sp overlap so less. Add random. Good.

```
 A=_build_a(...)
 if A.size==0: return None
 A=A/M
 b=np.ones(A.shape[0])
 res=opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,1.0), method='highs')
except Exception:
 return None
...
```

`_refine` line search and reset. Let's implement fully:
```
def _refine(p,n,deadline,rng,max_iters=1000,K=100):
    p=_normalize(p); p=p+_EPS; p/=p.sum()
    c=_conv_fft(p); J=float(c.max())
    best=p.copy(); bestJ=J
    no_imp=0; reset_count=0; lp_stale=0
    use_full = (K<=0)
    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
        active = _choose_active(c,n,rng,K)
        improved=False
        oldJ=J
        # LP
        if J > 1e-300 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12:
            g = _lp_solution(p, active, J*(1+1e-8)+1e-15, n)
            if g is not None:
                lp_stale=0
                for t in (0.5, 1.0, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
                    pnew = (1.0-t)*p + t*g
                    pnew = np.abs(pnew) + _EPS
                    pnew /= pnew.sum()
                    cnew = _conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < oldJ - 1e-15:
                        improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
                if not improved:
                    lp_stale += 1
        # gradient
        if not improved and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10 and (lp_stale >= 2 or not use_full or (not active.any())):
            h = _gradient_average(p, active, n)
            hmean = h.mean()
            d = h - hmean
            ds = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
            if ds > 1e-15:
                for eta in (1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
                    pnew = p * np.exp(-eta*d/ds)
                    pnew = np.abs(pnew)+_EPS
                    pnew /= pnew.sum()
                    cnew=_conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < J - 1e-15:
                        improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
        # perturb
        if not improved:
            for sigma in (0.10, 0.20, 0.05):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
                pnew = _perturb(p, rng, sigma)
                cnew=_conv_fft(pnew); Jnew=float(cnew.max())
                if Jnew < J - 1e-15:
                    improved=True; p=pnew; c=cnew; J=Jnew; break
        if improved:
            no_imp=0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        if J < bestJ - 1e-15:
            best=p.copy(); bestJ=J; no_imp=0
        else:
            if no_imp > 30:
                p = _perturb(best, rng, 0.25)
                c=_conv_fft(p); J=float(c.max())
                no_imp=0; reset_count +=1
                if reset_count > 12:
                    break
    return best, bestJ
```
Issue: If gradient uses active from old c and J updated by LP? If LP improved, improved True, skip. If LP not, J old. Good.
If LP returns g and t loop finds none, lp_stale++ and then gradient. Good.
If LP not called due time, gradient called if lp_stale>=2? lp_stale initially0, so condition `lp_stale>=2 or not use_full`? We want gradient always as fallback. So just call gradient if not improved and time, regardless of use_full. Remove weird condition.
```
if not improved and time<deadline-10:
   h=...
```
Good.

But gradient may be called even if LP tried and no improve; good.

If perturb improves from current J but current J maybe >bestJ; still current decreases; best updated if below best. Good.

If perturb no improve and no_imp>30, reset to best with perturb; J may be >best. Next iterations from perturbed best.

Potential bug: In `_refine`, after reset, `c` recompute but `J` maybe >best; best unchanged. Good.

`_gradient_average` uses active list; if active full all lags for n=256, gradient average over all lags. It is 2*(sum over all lags row)/m. Could be heavy: 511 slices for gradient if called. But in coarse, LP likely improves; gradient rarely. If called, 511*256 small.

```
def _initial_starts(n,parent,rng,extra=4):
 starts=[]
 if parent is not None:
    p=_upsample(parent,n)
    starts.append(p)
 t=...
 for a in (-0.8,0.8,-0.35,0.35): starts.append(...)
 for lam in (-3.5,3.5,-1.8,1.8): starts.append(exp)
 # one cos
 f=1+0.7*np.cos(4*np.pi*t); starts.append if min>0
 for i in range(extra): starts.append(_random_smooth(n,rng))
 # normalize and limit maybe 10
 out=[]
 for f in starts:
```

Let's write phase loop carefully to avoid using a variable before assignment.

```
prev_results=[]
free = max(0.0, hard_deadline - 25 - time.monotonic())
if free > 180:
   if n_f > 2048 and free < 900:
       n_f = min(n_f, 2048)
   if free > 900:
      phases=[(256,0.20,0,5), (1024,0.30,400,3), (n_f,0.50,120,3)]
   elif free > 450:
      phases=[(256,0.30,0,4), (n_f,0.70,120,2)]
   else:
      nf = min(n_f, 2048)
      phases=[(nf,1.0,100,2)]
   base_time=time.monotonic()
   acc=0.0
   for (n, frac, K, max_seeds) in phases:
       acc += frac
       phase_dead = min(hard_deadline - 20, base_time + acc*free)
       if time.monotonic() > phase_dead - 30: continue
       # if n too large for remaining phase
       phase_time = phase_dead - time.monotonic()
       if n > 4096 and phase_time < 250:
           n = min(n, 2048)
       if n > 2048 and phase_time < 120:
           n = min(n, 1024)
       if n < 64: n=64
       if not prev_results:
           extra = 3 if phase_time > 300 else 1
           seeds = _initial_starts(n, parent, rng, extra)
       else:
           sorted_prev = sorted(prev_results, key=lambda x: x[0])
           seeds=[]
           for _, pp in sorted_prev[:2]:
               seeds.append(_upsample(pp,n))
               seeds.append(_upsample(pp[::-1],n))
           if parent is not None:
               seeds.append(_upsample(parent,n))
           seeds.append(_random_smooth(n, rng, m=64))
       # remove None/empty, limit
       clean_seeds=[]
       for s in seeds:
          s=_normalize(s)
          if s.size==n and np.isfinite(s).all(): clean_seeds.append(s)
          if len(clean_seeds)>=max_seeds: break
       if not clean_seeds: clean_seeds=[_random_smooth(n,rng)]
       # optimize seeds
       results=[]
       for i,s in enumerate(clean_seeds):
           if time.monotonic() > phase_dead - 10: break
           rem = phase_dead - time.monotonic()
           per = max(15.0, rem / max(1, len(clean_seeds)-i) * 0.95)
           limit = min(phase_dead - 5, time.monotonic() + per)
           p,J = _refine(s, n, limit, rng, max_iters=100000, K=K)
           if p is not None and p.size==n:
               results.append((2*n*J, p.copy()))
       # exact update top 2
       if results:
           for _, pp in sorted(results, key=lambda x:x[0])[:2]:
               if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 15: break
               seq=[float(v) for v in pp]
               sc=_exact_score(seq)
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_seq=seq; best_score=sc
       prev_results = results
       # If phase too small or no results, break? continue next if time.
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as opt

_EPS = 1e-12
_IMPROVE_TOL = 1e-14

_INFO_CACHE = {}
_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}
_OBJ_CACHE = {}


def _slice_info(n):
    if n not in _INFO_CACHE:
        info = []
        for k in range(2 * n - 1):
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n, k + 1)
            if lo >= hi:
                info.append(None)
            else:
                start = k - lo
                end = k - (hi - 1)
                info.append((lo, hi, start, end, end == 0))
        _INFO_CACHE[n] = info
    return _INFO_CACHE[n]


def _fft_len(n):
    if n not in _FFT_LEN_CACHE:
        x = 2 * n - 1
        L = 1 << max(1, (x - 1).bit_length())
        _FFT_LEN_CACHE[n] = L
    return _FFT_LEN_CACHE[n]


def _obj_vec(n):
    if n not in _OBJ_CACHE:
        _OBJ_CACHE[n] = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _OBJ_CACHE[n]


def _conv_fft(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    L = _fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_ft(p):
    return float(_conv_fft(p).max())


def _normalize(p):
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.abs(arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / arr.size
    arr /= s
    return arr


def _safe_density(f):
    try:
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if f.size == 0:
        return None
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.abs(f) + 1e-8
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return f / s


def _clean_parent():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def _upsample(p, n_new):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if p.size == 0:
        return _safe_density(np.ones(n_new))
    if p.size == n_new:
        out = _normalize(p)
        return out if out is not None else _safe_density(np.ones(n_new))
    h = p * float(p.size)
    if h.size == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, None)
    x_old = (np.arange(h.size) + 0.5) / float(h.size)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) / float(n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    h_new = np.clip(h_new, 0.0, None) + 1e-12
    s = float(h_new.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    return h_new / s


def _random_smooth_dense(n, rng, m=None):
    n = int(n)
    if m is None:
        m = min(max(32, n // 16), 96)
    m = int(max(8, min(m, n)))
    x = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    y = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(3):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.75))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        y += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
    y = np.abs(y) + 0.05
    x_new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    f = np.interp(x_new, x, y)
    f = np.abs(f) + 1e-8
    f *= f
    return _safe_density(f)


def _initial_starts(n, parent, rng, extra=3):
    n = int(n)
    starts = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        starts.append(_upsample(parent, n))

    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)

    for a in (-0.8, 0.8, -0.35, 0.35):
        starts.append(_safe_density(1.0 + a * t))

    for lam in (-3.5, 3.5, -1.8, 1.8):
        starts.append(_safe_density(np.exp(lam * t)))

    starts.append(_safe_density(1.0 + 0.7 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t)))
    starts.append(_safe_density(1.0 + 0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)))

    extra = int(max(0, extra))
    for _ in range(extra):
        starts.append(_random_smooth_dense(n, rng))

    out = []
    for f in starts:
        g = _normalize(f)
        if g is not None and g.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            out.append(g)
            if len(out) >= 10:
                break
    if not out:
        out.append(_random_smooth_dense(n, rng))
    return out


def _apply_row(f, info, out):
    lo, hi, start, end, zero_end = info
    if zero_end:
        out[lo:hi] = f[start::-1]
    else:
        out[lo:hi] = f[start:end - 1:-1]


def _build_a(f, active, n):
    info = _slice_info(n)
    A = np.zeros((len(active), n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        inf = info[int(k)]
        if inf is None:
            continue
        _apply_row(f, inf, A[r])
    return A


def _gradient_average(f, active, n):
    info = _slice_info(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cnt = 0
    for k in active:
        inf = info[int(k)]
        if inf is None:
            continue
        _apply_row(f, inf, h)
        cnt += 1
    if cnt > 0:
        h /= float(cnt)
    return h


def _choose_active(c, n, rng, K=0):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if K <= 0 or K >= m:
        return np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)

    top_count = int(max(20, min(m // 2, K // 2)))
    top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_count:].astype(np.int64)

    rest = int(max(8, K - top_count))
    spread_count = int(max(8, rest // 2))
    random_count = int(max(8, rest - spread_count))

    spread_idx = np.linspace(0, m - 1, spread_count).astype(np.int64)
    if random_count > 0:
        rand_idx = rng.integers(0, m, random_count).astype(np.int64)
    else:
        rand_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

    act = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, spread_idx, rand_idx)))
    if act.size < K:
        extra_rng = int(max(0, K - act.size))
        act = np.unique(np.concatenate((act, rng.integers(0, m, extra_rng).astype(np.int64))))
    return act[:K].astype(np.int64)


def _lp_solution(f, active, M, n):
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    try:
        A = _build_a(f, active, n)
        if A.size == 0:
            return None
        A = A / M
        b = np.ones(A.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        obj = _obj_vec(n)
        try:
            res = opt.linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 1.0), method="highs")
        except Exception:
            res = opt.linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0, 1.0))
        if res is not None and res.success and res.x is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.abs(g)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                return g / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    n = int(p.size)
    m = min(max(32, n // 24), 96)
    m = max(8, min(m, n))
    x_old = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    x_new = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    noise = np.interp(x_new, x_old, rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m))
    q = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64) * np.exp(float(sigma) * noise)
    q = np.abs(q) + _EPS
    q /= float(q.sum())
    return q


def _refine(p, n, deadline, rng, max_iters=100000, K=100):
    n = int(n)
    p = _normalize(p)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = p + _EPS
    p /= float(p.sum())

    c = _conv_fft(p)
    J = float(c.max())
    best = p.copy()
    bestJ = J

    no_imp = 0
    reset_count = 0
    lp_stale = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        active = _choose_active(c, n, rng, K)
        improved = False
        oldJ = J

        if J > 1e-300 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
            g = _lp_solution(p, active, J * (1.0 + 1e-8) + 1e-15, n)
            if g is not None:
                lp_stale = 0
                for t in (0.5, 1.0, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                        break
                    pnew = (1.0 - t) * p + t * g
                    pnew = np.abs(pnew) + _EPS
                    pnew /= float(pnew.sum())
                    cnew = _conv_fft(pnew)
                    Jnew = float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < oldJ - _IMPROVE_TOL:
                        improved = True
                        p = pnew
                        c = cnew
                        J = Jnew
                        break
                if not improved:
                    lp_stale += 1
            else:
                lp_stale += 1

        if not improved and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
            h = _gradient_average(p, active, n)
            d = h - float(h.mean())
            ds = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
            if ds > 1e-15:
                for eta in (1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                        break
                    pnew = p * np.exp(-float(eta) * d / ds)
                    pnew = np.abs(pnew) + _EPS
                    pnew /= float(pnew.sum())
                    cnew = _conv_fft(pnew)
                    Jnew = float(cnew.max())
                    if Jnew < J - _IMPROVE_TOL:
                        improved = True
                        p = pnew
                        c = cnew
                        J = Jnew
                        break

        if not improved:
            for sigma in (0.10, 0.20, 0.05):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                    break
                pnew = _perturb(p, rng, sigma)
                cnew = _conv_fft(pnew)
                Jnew = float(cnew.max())
                if Jnew < J - _IMPROVE_TOL:
                    improved = True
                    p = pnew
                    c = cnew
                    J = Jnew
                    break

        if improved:
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if J < bestJ - _IMPROVE_TOL:
            best = p.copy()
            bestJ = J
            no_imp = 0
        elif no_imp > 30:
            p = _perturb(best, rng, 0.25)
            c = _conv_fft(p)
            J = float(c.max())
            no_imp = 0
            reset_count += 1
            if reset_count > 12:
                break

    return best, bestJ


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        s = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    return s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_reserve = 25.0
        hard_deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - total_reserve)
    except Exception:
        hard_deadline = start

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    parent = _clean_parent()

    best_seq = [1.0]
    best_score = 2.0

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 and parent.size <= 20000:
        if float(parent.sum()) >= 0.01:
            parent_list = [float(v) for v in parent]
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 5.0:
                pscore = _exact_score(parent_list)
                if np.isfinite(pscore) and pscore <= best_score + 1e-12:
                    if pscore < best_score - 1e-12 or len(parent_list) > len(best_seq):
                        best_seq = parent_list
                        best_score = pscore
            else:
                best_seq = parent_list
                best_score = 2.0

    n_final = 4096
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size >= 128:
            n_final = int(parent.size)
    if n_final > 10000:
        n_final = 6962
    if n_final < 128:
        n_final = 512

    free = max(0.0, hard_deadline - 25.0 - time.monotonic())
    prev_results = []

    if free > 180.0:
        if n_final > 2048 and free < 900.0:
            n_work = min(n_final, 2048)
        else:
            n_work = n_final

        if free > 900.0:
            phases = [
                (256, 0.20, 0, 5),
                (1024, 0.30, 300, 3),
                (n_work, 0.50, 90, 3),
            ]
        elif free > 450.0:
            phases = [
                (256, 0.30, 0, 4),
                (n_work, 0.70, 90, 2),
            ]
        else:
            phases = [
                (min(n_work, 2048), 1.0, 90, 2),
            ]

        base_t = time.monotonic()
        acc = 0.0

        for n_phase, frac, K, max_seeds in phases:
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            acc += float(frac)
            phase_dead = min(hard_deadline - 20.0, base_t + acc * free)
            phase_time = phase_dead - time.monotonic()

            if n_phase > 4096 and phase_time < 250.0:
                n_phase = min(n_phase, 2048)
            if n_phase > 2048 and phase_time < 120.0:
                n_phase = min(n_phase, 1024)
            n_phase = max(64, int(n_phase))

            if phase_time < 30.0:
                continue

            if not prev_results:
                extra_starts = 3 if phase_time > 300.0 else 1
                seeds = _initial_starts(n_phase, parent, rng, extra_starts)
            else:
                sorted_prev = sorted(prev_results, key=lambda x: x[0])
                seeds = []
                for _, pp in sorted_prev[:2]:
                    seeds.append(_upsample(pp, n_phase))
                    seeds.append(_upsample(pp[::-1], n_phase))
                if parent is not None:
                    seeds.append(_upsample(parent, n_phase))
                seeds.append(_random_smooth_dense(n_phase, rng, m=96 if n_phase > 2048 else 64))

            clean_seeds = []
            for s in seeds:
                ss = _normalize(s)
                if ss is not None and ss.size == n_phase and np.all(np.isfinite(ss)):
                    clean_seeds.append(ss)
                    if len(clean_seeds) >= int(max_seeds):
                        break
            if not clean_seeds:
                clean_seeds.append(_random_smooth_dense(n_phase, rng))

            results = []
            for i, s in enumerate(clean_seeds):
                if time.monotonic() > phase_dead - 10.0:
                    break
                rem = max(0.0, phase_dead - time.monotonic())
                per = max(15.0, rem / max(1, len(clean_seeds) - i) * 0.95)
                limit = min(phase_dead - 5.0, time.monotonic() + per)
                p_best, J_best = _refine(s, n_phase, limit, rng, max_iters=100000, K=K)
                if p_best is not None and p_best.size == n_phase and np.all(np.isfinite(p_best)):
                    results.append((2.0 * float(n_phase) * float(J_best), p_best.copy()))

            if results:
                for _, pp in sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[0])[:2]:
                    if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 15.0:
                        break
                    seq = [float(v) for v in pp]
                    sc = _exact_score(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_seq = seq
                        best_score = sc

            prev_results = results

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 5.0 and best_seq:
        try:
            sc = _exact_score([float(v) for v in best_seq])
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
            elif not np.isfinite(sc):
                best_seq = [1.0]
                best_score = 2.0
        except Exception:
            best_seq = [1.0]
            best_score = 2.0

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if arr.size == 0 or s <= 0.01:
            best_seq = [1.0]
        else:
            arr = arr / s
            best_seq = [float(v) for v in arr]
    except Exception:
        best_seq = [1.0]

    if not best_seq:
        best_seq = [1.0]

    return best_seq
```
